# #     """
# #     All application configuration loaded from environment variables.
# #     Uses pydantic-settings so values can come from .env file or real env vars.

# #     log_level: str = Field("INFO", env="LOG_LEVEL")

# #     # Cost / token ceilings per run

# # Module-level singleton so callers can do:

# Code Generated by Sidekick is for learning and experimentation purposes only.
"""
app/config.py â€” no changes required.
All settings referenced by node files are present and correctly named:
  CHECKPOINT_DB_PATH, DATA_ROOT, MAX_CLARIFICATION_ROUNDS,
  MAX_REFLECTION_ITERATIONS, MAX_CRITIC_ITERATIONS, DEFAULT_MODEL
"""
import json
from functools import lru_cache
from pydantic import Field
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings


class Settings(BaseSettings):

    # â”€â”€ OpenAI â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    OPENAI_API_KEY: str = Field(..., env="OPENAI_API_KEY")

    # â”€â”€ JWT â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    JWT_SECRET:         str = Field(...,      env="JWT_SECRET")
    JWT_ALGORITHM:      str = Field("HS256",  env="JWT_ALGORITHM")
    JWT_EXPIRE_MINUTES: int = Field(1440,     env="JWT_EXPIRE_MINUTES")

    # â”€â”€ Database paths â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    SQLITE_DB_PATH:     str = Field("./data/app.sqlite",         env="SQLITE_DB_PATH")
    CHECKPOINT_DB_PATH: str = Field("./data/checkpoints.sqlite", env="CHECKPOINT_DB_PATH")

    # â”€â”€ ChromaDB â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    CHROMA_PERSIST_DIR: str = Field("./data/chroma", env="CHROMA_PERSIST_DIR")

    # â”€â”€ File storage â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    DATA_ROOT: str = Field("./data/projects", env="DATA_ROOT")

    # â”€â”€ App â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    APP_ENV:   str = Field("development", env="APP_ENV")
    LOG_LEVEL: str = Field("INFO",        env="LOG_LEVEL")

    # â”€â”€ Cost / token ceilings â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    MAX_TOKENS_PER_RUN:   int   = Field(200000, env="MAX_TOKENS_PER_RUN")
    MAX_COST_USD_PER_RUN: float = Field(5.00,   env="MAX_COST_USD_PER_RUN")

    # â”€â”€ Workflow iteration limits â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    MAX_CLARIFICATION_ROUNDS:  int = Field(3, env="MAX_CLARIFICATION_ROUNDS")
    MAX_CRITIC_ITERATIONS:     int = Field(3, env="MAX_CRITIC_ITERATIONS")
    MAX_REVIEWER_RETRIES:      int = Field(3, env="MAX_REVIEWER_RETRIES")
    MAX_REFLECTION_ITERATIONS: int = Field(3, env="MAX_REFLECTION_ITERATIONS")

    # â”€â”€ Default LLM model â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    DEFAULT_MODEL: str = Field("gpt-4o", env="DEFAULT_MODEL")

    # â”€â”€ Model pricing â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    MODEL_PRICING_RAW: str = Field(
        '{"gpt-4o":{"input":0.005,"output":0.015}}',
        env="MODEL_PRICING",
    )

    @property
    def MODEL_PRICING(self) -> dict:
        return json.loads(self.MODEL_PRICING_RAW)

    class Config:
        env_file          = ".env"
        env_file_encoding = "utf-8"
        extra             = "ignore"


@lru_cache()
def get_settings() -> Settings:
    return Settings()


settings = get_settings()

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import HTTPBearer, HTTPAuthorizationCredentials
from jose import JWTError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.storage.database import get_db
from app.auth.service import decode_token, get_user_by_id
from app.auth.models import User
from app.auth.schemas import TokenData

# FastAPI's built-in Bearer token extractor from the Authorization header
bearer_scheme = HTTPBearer()


async def get_current_user(
    credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(bearer_scheme),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> User:
    """
    FastAPI dependency used on every protected route.
    Extracts the Bearer token, decodes it, and returns the User from DB.
    Returns 401 if the token is missing, expired, or invalid.
    """
    token = credentials.credentials

    try:
        token_data: TokenData = decode_token(token)
    except JWTError:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail={"code": "INVALID_TOKEN", "message": "Token is invalid or expired"},
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

    user = await get_user_by_id(db, token_data.sub)
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail={"code": "USER_NOT_FOUND", "message": "User associated with token no longer exists"},
        )

    return user

# app = FastAPI(
#     title="AI Agent Factory",
#     description=(
#         "Backend-only AI agent factory that turns BRD/PRD/TRD documents into "
#         "working code through three sequential autonomous agentic workflows.\n\n"
#         # â”€â”€ M3 ADD: document WS protocol in OpenAPI description â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
#         "## WebSocket HITL Channel\n"
#         "**Endpoint:** `WS /projects/{project_id}/runs/{run_id}/hitl?token=<JWT>`\n\n"
#         "**Protocol (server-initiated only):**\n"
#         "| Direction | Message type | When |\n"
#         "|-----------|-------------|------|\n"
#         "| Server â†’ Client | `clarification_request` | Agent finds a gap |\n"
#         "| Client â†’ Server | `clarification_response` | User answers questions |\n"
#         "| Server â†’ Client | `approval_request` | Workflow output ready for review |\n"
#         "| Client â†’ Server | `approval_response` | User approves or rejects |\n"
#         "| Server â†’ Client | `clarification_failed` | Cap exceeded |\n"
#         "| Server â†’ Client | `run_completed` | Workflow finished |\n"
#         "| Server â†’ Client | `error` | Unrecoverable error |\n"
#         "| Server â†’ Client | `ping` | Liveness check (every 20 s) |\n"
#         "| Client â†’ Server | `pong` | Must reply within 10 s |\n\n"
#         "**Error semantics:**\n"
#         "- `4401` â€” missing/invalid JWT\n"
#         "- `4404` â€” project or run not found\n"
#         "- `4408` â€” pong timeout\n"
#         "- `4409` â€” second connection (first is closed)\n"
#         "- `4400` â€” unsolicited client message\n\n"
#         "**Idempotency:** Each `*_request` has a stable `request_id`. "
#         "On reconnect the server re-pushes the pending request. "
#         "Duplicate `*_response` messages for the same `request_id` are ignored.\n\n"
#         "**REST equivalents** exist for every WebSocket interaction "
#         "(see `/projects/{id}/runs/{id}/clarifications`, "
#         "`/projects/{id}/runs/{id}/approve`, "
#         "`/projects/{id}/runs/{id}/reject`)."
#     ),
#     version="1.1.0",   # bumped for Milestone 3
#     lifespan=lifespan,
#     swagger_ui_init_oauth={},
# )

import os
import logging

import structlog
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from app.config import get_settings
from app.storage.database import (
    init_db,
    AsyncSessionLocal,
    init_checkpointer,      # â† NEW
    close_checkpointer,     # â† NEW
)
from app.auth.router import router as auth_router
from app.projects.router import router as projects_router
from app.documents.router import router as documents_router
from app.patterns.router import router as patterns_router

from app.patterns.seed import seed_patterns
from app.routers.planning import router as planning_router

settings = get_settings()

structlog.configure(
    processors=[
        structlog.processors.TimeStamper(fmt="iso"),
        structlog.stdlib.add_log_level,
        structlog.processors.JSONRenderer(),
    ],
    wrapper_class=structlog.stdlib.BoundLogger,
    logger_factory=structlog.PrintLoggerFactory(),
)

log = structlog.get_logger()


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# LIFESPAN
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    log.info("Starting Agent Factory API", env=settings.APP_ENV)

    # â”€â”€ Ensure root data directories exist â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    os.makedirs(settings.DATA_ROOT, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(settings.SQLITE_DB_PATH), exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(settings.DATA_ROOT, "projects"),    exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(settings.DATA_ROOT, "graphs"),      exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(settings.DATA_ROOT, "checkpoints"), exist_ok=True)

    checkpoint_dir = os.path.dirname(settings.CHECKPOINT_DB_PATH)
    if checkpoint_dir:
        os.makedirs(checkpoint_dir, exist_ok=True)

    # â”€â”€ Initialize app database â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    await init_db()
    log.info("Database initialized")

    # â”€â”€ Initialize checkpointer (MUST be before any workflow can run) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    # This is the ONLY place the aiosqlite connection is opened.
    # All workflow runners call get_checkpointer() to reuse this connection.
    await init_checkpointer()
    log.info("Checkpointer initialized", path=settings.CHECKPOINT_DB_PATH)

    # â”€â”€ Seed Pattern KB â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        await seed_patterns(db)
    log.info("Pattern KB seeded")

    log.info(
        "In-memory run-slot registry active",
        note=(
            "Run locks and SSE channels are in-memory (single-process only). "
            "State is lost on restart. Production: use Redis or distributed store."
        ),
    )

    yield  # â”€â”€ app is running â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

    # â”€â”€ Shutdown â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    await close_checkpointer()
    log.info("Shutting down Agent Factory API")


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# APP FACTORY
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

app = FastAPI(
    title="AI Agent Factory",
    description=(
        "Backend-only AI agent factory that turns BRD/PRD/TRD documents into "
        "working code through three sequential autonomous agentic workflows.\n\n"
        "## WebSocket HITL Channel\n"
        "**Endpoint:** `WS /projects/{project_id}/runs/{run_id}/hitl?token=<JWT>`\n\n"
        "**Protocol (server-initiated only):**\n"
        "| Direction | Message type | When |\n"
        "|-----------|-------------|------|\n"
        "| Server â†’ Client | `clarification_request` | Agent finds a gap |\n"
        "| Client â†’ Server | `clarification_response` | User answers questions |\n"
        "| Server â†’ Client | `approval_request` | Workflow output ready for review |\n"
        "| Client â†’ Server | `approval_response` | User approves or rejects |\n"
        "| Server â†’ Client | `clarification_failed` | Cap exceeded |\n"
        "| Server â†’ Client | `run_completed` | Workflow finished |\n"
        "| Server â†’ Client | `error` | Unrecoverable error |\n"
        "| Server â†’ Client | `ping` | Liveness check (every 20 s) |\n"
        "| Client â†’ Server | `pong` | Must reply within 10 s |\n\n"
        "**Error semantics:**\n"
        "- `4401` â€” missing/invalid JWT\n"
        "- `4404` â€” project or run not found\n"
        "- `4408` â€” pong timeout\n"
        "- `4409` â€” second connection (first is closed)\n"
        "- `4400` â€” unsolicited client message\n\n"
        "**Idempotency:** Each `*_request` has a stable `request_id`. "
        "On reconnect the server re-pushes the pending request. "
        "Duplicate `*_response` messages for the same `request_id` are ignored.\n\n"
        "**REST equivalents** exist for every WebSocket interaction "
        "(see `/projects/{id}/runs/{id}/clarifications`, "
        "`/projects/{id}/runs/{id}/approve`, "
        "`/projects/{id}/runs/{id}/reject`)."
    ),
    version="1.1.0",
    lifespan=lifespan,
    swagger_ui_init_oauth={},
)

# â”€â”€ CORS â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"] if settings.APP_ENV == "development" else [],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# GLOBAL ERROR HANDLER
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

@app.exception_handler(Exception)
async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: Exception):
    log.error("Unhandled exception", path=request.url.path, error=str(exc))
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=500,
        content={
            "error": {
                "code":    "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                "message": str(exc),
                "details": None,
            }
        },
    )


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# HEALTH CHECK
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

@app.get("/healthz", tags=["Health"], summary="Health check")
async def health_check():
    from app.storage.database import engine
    from app.storage.vector_store import get_chroma_client

    checks: dict = {}

    try:
        async with engine.connect() as conn:
            await conn.execute(__import__("sqlalchemy").text("SELECT 1"))
        checks["sqlite"] = "ok"
    except Exception as e:
        checks["sqlite"] = f"error: {e}"

    try:
        import aiosqlite
        async with aiosqlite.connect(settings.CHECKPOINT_DB_PATH) as conn:
            await conn.execute("SELECT 1")
        checks["sqlite_checkpoints"] = "ok"
    except Exception as e:
        checks["sqlite_checkpoints"] = f"error: {e}"

    try:
        client = get_chroma_client()
        client.heartbeat()
        checks["chromadb"] = "ok"
    except Exception as e:
        checks["chromadb"] = f"error: {e}"

    try:
        os.makedirs(settings.DATA_ROOT, exist_ok=True)
        checks["filesystem"] = "ok"
    except Exception as e:
        checks["filesystem"] = f"error: {e}"

    all_ok = all(v == "ok" for v in checks.values())

    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=200 if all_ok else 503,
        content={
            "status": "healthy" if all_ok else "degraded",
            "checks": checks,
        },
    )


# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# WORKFLOW GRAPH PNG ENDPOINTS
# â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

from fastapi.responses import Response as FastAPIResponse


@app.get(
    "/workflows/requirements/graph.png",
    tags=["Workflows & Runs"],
    summary="Rendered Mermaid diagram of the Requirements workflow graph",
    response_class=FastAPIResponse,
    description=(
        "Returns a PNG render of the Requirements Gathering workflow graph. "
        "Cached to disk after the first render."
    ),
)
async def requirements_graph_png():
    cache_path = os.path.join(settings.DATA_ROOT, "graphs", "requirements.png")
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(cache_path), exist_ok=True)

    if not os.path.isfile(cache_path):
        try:
            from app.workflows.requirements.graph import get_graph_png_bytes
            png_bytes = get_graph_png_bytes()
            with open(cache_path, "wb") as f:
                f.write(png_bytes)
        except Exception as e:
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=500,
                content={
                    "error": {
                        "code":    "GRAPH_RENDER_ERROR",
                        "message": str(e),
                        "details": (
                            "Ensure mermaid-js or graphviz is installed. "
                            "Run: pip install grandalf  or  npm install -g @mermaid-js/mermaid-cli"
                        ),
                    }
                },
            )

    with open(cache_path, "rb") as f:
        png_bytes = f.read()

    return FastAPIResponse(content=png_bytes, media_type="image/png")

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Django's command-line utility for administrative tasks."""
import os
import sys


def main():
    """Run administrative tasks."""
    os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'myproject.settings')
    try:
        from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

# Code Generated by Sidekick is for learning and experimentation purposes only.

"""
app/auth/dependencies.py
â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
FastAPI dependency that validates the Bearer JWT on every protected route
and returns the authenticated User ORM object.

Usage in any router:
    from app.auth.dependencies import get_current_user
    ...
    async def my_endpoint(current_user = Depends(get_current_user)):
"""

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer
from jose import JWTError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.storage.database import get_db
from app.auth import service as auth_service
from app.auth.models import User

# Points to your existing /auth/login endpoint â€” tells Swagger where to get a token
oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl="/auth/login")


async def get_current_user(
    token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> User:
    """
    Dependency injected into protected routes.

    1. Extracts the Bearer token from the Authorization header
    2. Decodes + validates the JWT via auth_service.decode_token()
    3. Loads the User row from the DB
    4. Returns the User â€” or raises 401 if anything fails
    """
    credentials_exception = HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
        detail={"code": "INVALID_TOKEN", "message": "Could not validate credentials"},
        headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
    )

    try:
        # decode_token() raises JWTError if token is expired or tampered
        token_data = auth_service.decode_token(token)
    except JWTError:
        raise credentials_exception

    # Load the full User row so routes have access to user.id, user.username, etc.
    user = await auth_service.get_user_by_id(db, token_data.sub)

    if user is None:
        raise credentials_exception

    return user

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from sqlalchemy import String, DateTime
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column
from app.storage.database import Base


class User(Base):
    """
    Single user account. The whole system is single-user,
    but we still store the user in DB so the JWT subject (sub) maps to a real row.
    """
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String, primary_key=True)          # UUID string
    username: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String, unique=True, nullable=False)
    hashed_password: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String, nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    )

# Code Generated by Sidekick is for learning and experimentation purposes only.
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status, Form
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordRequestForm
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from typing import Optional

from app.storage.database import get_db
from app.auth.schemas import RegisterRequest, TokenResponse
from app.auth import service as auth_service

router = APIRouter(prefix="/auth", tags=["Authentication"])


@router.post(
    "/register",
    response_model=TokenResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="Register a new user (dev-only)"
)
async def register(body: RegisterRequest, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
    """
    Create a new user account and return a JWT immediately.
    In production this endpoint would be disabled or admin-gated.
    """
    existing = await auth_service.get_user_by_username(db, body.username)
    if existing:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail={"code": "USERNAME_TAKEN", "message": "Username already exists"}
        )

    user = await auth_service.create_user(db, body.username, body.password)
    token, expires_in = auth_service.create_access_token(user.id, user.username)
    return TokenResponse(access_token=token, expires_in=expires_in)


@router.post(
    "/login",
    response_model=TokenResponse,
    summary="Login and receive a JWT bearer token"
)
async def login(
    username: str = Form(...),
    password: str = Form(...),
    grant_type: Optional[str] = Form(default=None),
    scope: Optional[str] = Form(default=""),
    client_id: Optional[str] = Form(default=None),       # â† Optional, no longer mandatory
    client_secret: Optional[str] = Form(default=None),   # â† Optional, no longer mandatory
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    Validate credentials and return a signed JWT.
    Use this token in the Authorization: Bearer <token> header for all other calls.
    """
    user = await auth_service.get_user_by_username(db, username)

    if not user or not auth_service.verify_password(password, user.hashed_password):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail={"code": "INVALID_CREDENTIALS", "message": "Username or password is incorrect"},
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )

    token, expires_in = auth_service.create_access_token(user.id, user.username)
    return TokenResponse(access_token=token, expires_in=expires_in)

from pydantic import BaseModel


class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
    """Body for POST /auth/login"""
    username: str
    password: str


class RegisterRequest(BaseModel):
    """Body for POST /auth/register (dev-only)"""
    username: str
    password: str


class TokenResponse(BaseModel):
    """Returned on successful login"""
    access_token: str
    token_type: str = "bearer"
    expires_in: int   # seconds until expiry


class TokenData(BaseModel):
    """Parsed claims from a validated JWT"""
    sub: str          # user_id
    username: str

# Code Generated by Sidekick is for learning and experimentation purposes only.

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Optional
import uuid

from jose import jwt, JWTError
from passlib.context import CryptContext
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.config import get_settings
from app.auth.models import User
from app.auth.schemas import TokenData

settings = get_settings()

# bcrypt context for hashing passwords
pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")


def hash_password(plain: str) -> str:
    """Hash a plain-text password using bcrypt."""
    return pwd_context.hash(plain)


def verify_password(plain: str, hashed: str) -> bool:
    """Check a plain password against a stored bcrypt hash."""
    return pwd_context.verify(plain, hashed)


def create_access_token(user_id: str, username: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
    """
    Issue a signed JWT.
    Returns (token_string, expires_in_seconds).
    """
    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=settings.JWT_EXPIRE_MINUTES)
    payload = {
        "sub": user_id,
        "username": username,
        "iat": datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        "exp": expire,
    }
    token = jwt.encode(payload, settings.JWT_SECRET, algorithm=settings.JWT_ALGORITHM)
    return token, settings.JWT_EXPIRE_MINUTES * 60


def decode_token(token: str) -> TokenData:
    """
    Validate and decode a JWT.
    Raises JWTError if invalid or expired.
    """
    payload = jwt.decode(
        token,
        settings.JWT_SECRET,
        algorithms=[settings.JWT_ALGORITHM]
    )
    return TokenData(sub=payload["sub"], username=payload["username"])


async def get_user_by_username(db: AsyncSession, username: str) -> Optional[User]:
    """Fetch a user by username from the DB."""
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.username == username))
    return result.scalar_one_or_none()


async def get_user_by_id(db: AsyncSession, user_id: str) -> Optional[User]:
    """Fetch a user by ID from the DB."""
    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == user_id))
    return result.scalar_one_or_none()


async def create_user(db: AsyncSession, username: str, password: str) -> User:
    """Create and persist a new user with a hashed password."""
    user = User(
        id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
        username=username,
        hashed_password=hash_password(password),
    )
    db.add(user)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(user)
    return user

import enum
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from sqlalchemy import String, Integer, DateTime, Enum as SAEnum, ForeignKey, Text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship
from app.storage.database import Base


class ParseStatus(str, enum.Enum):
    """
    Tracks where a document is in the parse pipeline.

    pending   â†’ file saved, parse job not started yet
    parsing   â†’ background job is actively parsing
    ready     â†’ parsed, chunked, embedded â€” ready for RAG
    failed    â†’ parse job threw an error
    """
    PENDING  = "pending"
    PARSING  = "parsing"
    READY    = "ready"
    FAILED   = "failed"


class DocumentKind(str, enum.Enum):
    """
    What type of document this is.
    Stored in chunk metadata so agents can filter by document type.
    e.g., 'give me only requirements from the BRD'
    """
    BRD   = "BRD"
    PRD   = "PRD"
    TRD   = "TRD"
    OTHER = "other"


class Document(Base):
    """
    Represents one uploaded file.
    Each document belongs to exactly one project (non-null project_id FK).
    """
    __tablename__ = "documents"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String, primary_key=True)

    # Every document is scoped to a project â€” cross-project access returns 404
    project_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String,
        ForeignKey("projects.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
        nullable=False,
        index=True,
    )

    # Original filename the user uploaded (e.g., "requirements.pdf")
    filename: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)

    # File extension without the dot (e.g., "pdf", "docx")
    extension: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=False)

    # MIME type (e.g., "application/pdf")
    mime_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=False)

    # File size in bytes â€” used to enforce upload size limits
    file_size: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)

    # SHA-256 hash of the raw file content â€” used for idempotent uploads
    content_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False, index=True)

    # Absolute path to the raw file on disk
    file_path: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500), nullable=False)

    # What kind of document this is (BRD / PRD / TRD / other)
    kind: Mapped[DocumentKind] = mapped_column(
        SAEnum(DocumentKind),
        default=DocumentKind.OTHER,
        nullable=False,
    )

    # Current state of the parse pipeline
    status: Mapped[ParseStatus] = mapped_column(
        SAEnum(ParseStatus),
        default=ParseStatus.PENDING,
        nullable=False,
    )

    # How many logical sections were extracted (filled after parsing)
    section_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)

    # How many chunks were embedded into ChromaDB (filled after embedding)
    chunk_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)

    # If status=failed, the error message is stored here
    error_message: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        nullable=False,
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        onupdate=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        nullable=False,
    )

    # Relationships
    project: Mapped["Project"] = relationship("Project", back_populates="documents")  # noqa: F821
    chunks: Mapped[list["DocumentChunk"]] = relationship(
        "DocumentChunk",
        back_populates="document",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan",
    )


class DocumentChunk(Base):
    """
    One logical chunk of a parsed document.

    Why store chunks in SQL AND ChromaDB?
    - SQL: structured metadata, section outline, fast lookup by document_id
    - ChromaDB: vector embeddings for semantic similarity search

    They are kept in sync â€” a chunk row in SQL has a matching vector in ChromaDB
    with the same section_id.
    """
    __tablename__ = "document_chunks"

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String, primary_key=True)

    document_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String,
        ForeignKey("documents.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
        nullable=False,
        index=True,
    )

    # project_id is denormalized here for fast scoped queries
    project_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String, nullable=False, index=True)

    # Stable ID for this chunk â€” used as the ChromaDB vector ID
    # Format: {document_id}_{section_index}
    section_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=False)

    # Heading or title of this section (empty string if not detected)
    section_title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(500), nullable=False, default="")

    # The actual text content of this chunk
    text: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False)

    # Page number where this chunk starts (0 for non-paginated formats like MD/TXT)
    page: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)

    # Position of this chunk within the document (for ordering)
    chunk_index: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)

    # Document kind copied from parent document (for ChromaDB filter convenience)
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10), nullable=False, default="other")

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        nullable=False,
    )

    # Relationship back to the parent document
    document: Mapped["Document"] = relationship("Document", back_populates="chunks")

# app/documents/parser.py
# Code Generated by Sidekick is for learning and experimentation purposes only.

import io
import re
from typing import BinaryIO


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Output format                                                         #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def make_chunk(
    section_id: str,
    text: str,
    section_title: str = "",
    page: int = 0,
    chunk_index: int = 0,
) -> dict:
    """
    Helper to build a consistent chunk dict.
    Every parser returns a list of these dicts.
    """
    return {
        "section_id":    section_id,
        "text":          text.strip(),
        "section_title": section_title.strip(),
        "page":          page,
        "chunk_index":   chunk_index,
    }


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Text splitting                                                        #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def split_text_into_chunks(
    text: str,
    chunk_size: int = 1000,
    chunk_overlap: int = 100,
) -> list[str]:
    """
    Splits a long text string into overlapping chunks.

    Why overlap? So that a sentence that falls at a chunk boundary
    appears in both chunks â€” this improves RAG recall.

    chunk_size    = max characters per chunk
    chunk_overlap = how many characters the next chunk shares with the previous
    """
    chunks = []
    start = 0

    while start < len(text):
        end = start + chunk_size

        # Try to break at a sentence boundary (. ! ?) rather than mid-word
        if end < len(text):
            # Look backwards from 'end' for the last sentence-ending punctuation
            break_point = max(
                text.rfind(". ", start, end),
                text.rfind("? ", start, end),
                text.rfind("! ", start, end),
                text.rfind("\n", start, end),
            )
            if break_point > start + (chunk_size // 2):
                end = break_point + 1  # include the punctuation

        chunk = text[start:end].strip()
        if chunk:
            chunks.append(chunk)

        # Move forward, stepping back by overlap amount
        start = end - chunk_overlap

    return chunks


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# PDF Parser                                                            #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def parse_pdf(file_bytes: bytes) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Extract text from a PDF file, page by page.
    Each page becomes one or more chunks.
    We use pypdf which handles most standard PDFs.
    """
    from pypdf import PdfReader

    reader = PdfReader(io.BytesIO(file_bytes))
    chunks = []
    chunk_index = 0

    for page_num, page in enumerate(reader.pages):
        page_text = page.extract_text() or ""
        page_text = page_text.strip()

        if not page_text:
            # Skip empty pages (cover pages, blank pages, etc.)
            continue

        # Split each page into sub-chunks if it's long
        sub_chunks = split_text_into_chunks(page_text)

        for sub_idx, sub_text in enumerate(sub_chunks):
            if not sub_text:
                continue

            # Try to detect a heading on this page (first line often is the heading)
            lines = sub_text.split("\n")
            title = lines[0][:100] if lines else f"Page {page_num + 1}"

            chunks.append(make_chunk(
                section_id=f"page{page_num + 1}_chunk{sub_idx}",
                text=sub_text,
                section_title=title,
                page=page_num + 1,
                chunk_index=chunk_index,
            ))
            chunk_index += 1

    return chunks


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# DOCX Parser                                                           #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def parse_docx(file_bytes: bytes) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Extract text from a Word document (.docx).
    Word documents have explicit paragraph styles â€” we use Heading styles
    to detect section boundaries, which gives us clean section_title values.
    """
    from docx import Document

    doc = Document(io.BytesIO(file_bytes))
    chunks = []
    chunk_index = 0

    # We accumulate paragraphs under each heading into a section buffer
    current_heading = "Introduction"
    current_text_parts = []
    section_num = 0

    def flush_section(heading: str, text_parts: list[str], sec_num: int) -> list[dict]:
        """
        Take the accumulated text for one section and split it into chunks.
        Returns a list of chunk dicts.
        """
        full_text = "\n".join(text_parts).strip()
        if not full_text:
            return []

        result = []
        sub_chunks = split_text_into_chunks(full_text)
        for sub_idx, sub_text in enumerate(sub_chunks):
            if sub_text:
                result.append(make_chunk(
                    section_id=f"section{sec_num}_chunk{sub_idx}",
                    text=sub_text,
                    section_title=heading,
                    page=0,   # DOCX doesn't expose page numbers easily
                    chunk_index=0,  # will be re-indexed in the main loop
                ))
        return result

    for para in doc.paragraphs:
        style_name = para.style.name if para.style else ""
        text = para.text.strip()

        if not text:
            continue  # skip empty paragraphs

        # Word heading styles are named "Heading 1", "Heading 2", etc.
        is_heading = style_name.startswith("Heading")

        if is_heading:
            # Save the previous section before starting a new one
            flushed = flush_section(current_heading, current_text_parts, section_num)
            for c in flushed:
                c["chunk_index"] = chunk_index
                chunks.append(c)
                chunk_index += 1

            # Start a new section with this heading
            current_heading = text
            current_text_parts = []
            section_num += 1
        else:
            current_text_parts.append(text)

    # Don't forget the last section
    flushed = flush_section(current_heading, current_text_parts, section_num)
    for c in flushed:
        c["chunk_index"] = chunk_index
        chunks.append(c)
        chunk_index += 1

    return chunks


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# PPTX Parser                                                           #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def parse_pptx(file_bytes: bytes) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Extract text from a PowerPoint file (.pptx).
    Each slide becomes one chunk. The slide title (if present) becomes section_title.
    """
    from pptx import Presentation

    prs = Presentation(io.BytesIO(file_bytes))
    chunks = []
    chunk_index = 0

    for slide_num, slide in enumerate(prs.slides):
        slide_title = ""
        slide_texts = []

        for shape in slide.shapes:
            if not shape.has_text_frame:
                continue

            # The title placeholder usually has shape.shape_type == 13
            # but checking the placeholder type is more reliable
            is_title = (
                hasattr(shape, "placeholder_format")
                and shape.placeholder_format is not None
                and shape.placeholder_format.idx == 0  # 0 = title placeholder
            )

            shape_text = "\n".join(
                para.text.strip()
                for para in shape.text_frame.paragraphs
                if para.text.strip()
            )

            if is_title:
                slide_title = shape_text
            elif shape_text:
                slide_texts.append(shape_text)

        full_slide_text = "\n".join(slide_texts).strip()

        if not full_slide_text and not slide_title:
            continue  # skip blank slides

        # Combine title + body for the chunk text
        combined = f"{slide_title}\n\n{full_slide_text}".strip() if slide_title else full_slide_text

        # Slides are usually short enough to be a single chunk
        sub_chunks = split_text_into_chunks(combined)
        for sub_idx, sub_text in enumerate(sub_chunks):
            if sub_text:
                chunks.append(make_chunk(
                    section_id=f"slide{slide_num + 1}_chunk{sub_idx}",
                    text=sub_text,
                    section_title=slide_title or f"Slide {slide_num + 1}",
                    page=slide_num + 1,
                    chunk_index=chunk_index,
                ))
                chunk_index += 1

    return chunks


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# XLSX Parser                                                           #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def parse_xlsx(file_bytes: bytes) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Extract text from an Excel file (.xlsx).
    Each sheet becomes one chunk. We convert the sheet's cells into
    a readable text representation (header row + data rows).
    """
    import openpyxl

    wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(io.BytesIO(file_bytes), read_only=True, data_only=True)
    chunks = []
    chunk_index = 0

    for sheet_num, sheet_name in enumerate(wb.sheetnames):
        ws = wb[sheet_name]
        rows_text = []

        for row in ws.iter_rows(values_only=True):
            # Convert each cell to string, skip rows that are completely empty
            row_values = [str(cell) if cell is not None else "" for cell in row]
            row_text = " | ".join(row_values).strip()
            if any(cell for cell in row_values):  # skip all-empty rows
                rows_text.append(row_text)

        if not rows_text:
            continue  # skip empty sheets

        sheet_text = "\n".join(rows_text)
        sub_chunks = split_text_into_chunks(sheet_text)

        for sub_idx, sub_text in enumerate(sub_chunks):
            if sub_text:
                chunks.append(make_chunk(
                    section_id=f"sheet{sheet_num + 1}_chunk{sub_idx}",
                    text=sub_text,
                    section_title=sheet_name,
                    page=0,
                    chunk_index=chunk_index,
                ))
                chunk_index += 1

    wb.close()
    return chunks


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Markdown Parser                                                       #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def parse_markdown(file_bytes: bytes) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Parse a Markdown file by splitting on heading lines (# ## ###).
    Each heading introduces a new section. This gives us clean, meaningful
    section boundaries for RAG â€” better than arbitrary character splits.
    """
    text = file_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    chunks = []
    chunk_index = 0

    # Split the text by Markdown headings using regex
    # Pattern: a line starting with one or more # characters
    heading_pattern = re.compile(r"^(#{1,6})\s+(.+)$", re.MULTILINE)

    # Find all heading positions
    heading_matches = list(heading_pattern.finditer(text))

    if not heading_matches:
        # No headings found â€” treat whole file as one section
        sub_chunks = split_text_into_chunks(text.strip())
        for sub_idx, sub_text in enumerate(sub_chunks):
            if sub_text:
                chunks.append(make_chunk(
                    section_id=f"section0_chunk{sub_idx}",
                    text=sub_text,
                    section_title="Document",
                    page=0,
                    chunk_index=chunk_index,
                ))
                chunk_index += 1
        return chunks

    # Process text before the first heading (preamble)
    preamble = text[:heading_matches[0].start()].strip()
    if preamble:
        sub_chunks = split_text_into_chunks(preamble)
        for sub_idx, sub_text in enumerate(sub_chunks):
            if sub_text:
                chunks.append(make_chunk(
                    section_id=f"preamble_chunk{sub_idx}",
                    text=sub_text,
                    section_title="Preamble",
                    page=0,
                    chunk_index=chunk_index,
                ))
                chunk_index += 1

    # Process each heading + its body text
    for i, match in enumerate(heading_matches):
        heading_text = match.group(2).strip()

        # Body text = from end of this heading line to start of next heading
        body_start = match.end()
        body_end = heading_matches[i + 1].start() if i + 1 < len(heading_matches) else len(text)
        body_text = text[body_start:body_end].strip()

        if not body_text:
            continue  # skip headings with no content

        sub_chunks = split_text_into_chunks(body_text)
        for sub_idx, sub_text in enumerate(sub_chunks):
            if sub_text:
                chunks.append(make_chunk(
                    section_id=f"section{i + 1}_chunk{sub_idx}",
                    text=sub_text,
                    section_title=heading_text,
                    page=0,
                    chunk_index=chunk_index,
                ))
                chunk_index += 1

    return chunks


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# TXT Parser                                                            #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

def parse_txt(file_bytes: bytes) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Parse a plain text file.
    No structure to detect, so we just split into fixed-size chunks.
    We try to detect "paragraph breaks" (double newlines) as soft boundaries.
    """
    text = file_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip()

    if not text:
        return []

    chunks = []
    chunk_index = 0

    # Split on double newlines first (paragraph boundaries)
    paragraphs = re.split(r"\n\s*\n", text)
    buffer = ""

    for para in paragraphs:
        para = para.strip()
        if not para:
            continue

        buffer = (buffer + "\n\n" + para).strip() if buffer else para

        # Once the buffer is large enough, flush it as a chunk
        if len(buffer) >= 800:
            sub_chunks = split_text_into_chunks(buffer)
            for sub_idx, sub_text in enumerate(sub_chunks):
                if sub_text:
                    chunks.append(make_chunk(
                        section_id=f"para_chunk{chunk_index}_{sub_idx}",
                        text=sub_text,
                        section_title=f"Section {chunk_index + 1}",
                        page=0,
                        chunk_index=chunk_index,
                    ))
            chunk_index += 1
            buffer = ""

    # Flush any remaining text
    if buffer:
        sub_chunks = split_text_into_chunks(buffer)
        for sub_idx, sub_text in enumerate(sub_chunks):
            if sub_text:
                chunks.append(make_chunk(
                    section_id=f"para_chunk{chunk_index}_{sub_idx}",
                    text=sub_text,
                    section_title=f"Section {chunk_index + 1}",
                    page=0,
                    chunk_index=chunk_index,
                ))

    return chunks


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Main dispatcher                                                       #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

# Maps file extension â†’ parser function
PARSERS = {
    "pdf":  parse_pdf,
    "docx": parse_docx,
    "pptx": parse_pptx,
    "xlsx": parse_xlsx,
    "md":   parse_markdown,
    "txt":  parse_txt,
}

# Supported MIME types and their extensions
SUPPORTED_MIME_TYPES = {
    "application/pdf":                                                  "pdf",
    "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document": "docx",
    "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation": "pptx",
    "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet": "xlsx",
    "text/markdown":  "md",
    "text/x-markdown": "md",
    "text/plain":     "txt",
}

# Max file size: 50MB
MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024


def get_extension_from_mime(mime_type: str) -> str | None:
    """
    Map a MIME type to a file extension.
    Returns None if the MIME type is not supported.
    """
    return SUPPORTED_MIME_TYPES.get(mime_type)


def parse_document(file_bytes: bytes, extension: str) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Main entry point for parsing.
    Dispatches to the correct parser based on file extension.
    Returns a list of chunk dicts.

    Raises ValueError if the extension is not supported.
    """
    parser_fn = PARSERS.get(extension.lower())

    if not parser_fn:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported file extension: {extension}")

    # Run the parser and filter out any empty chunks
    raw_chunks = parser_fn(file_bytes)
    return [c for c in raw_chunks if c["text"].strip()]

# app/documents/router.py
# Code Generated by Sidekick is for learning and experimentation purposes only.

from fastapi import (
    APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, UploadFile, File,
    BackgroundTasks, Query, status
)
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.storage.database import get_db
from app.dependencies import get_current_user
from app.auth.models import User
from app.projects import service as project_service
from app.documents import service as doc_service
from app.documents.models import DocumentKind, ParseStatus
from app.documents.parser import (
    SUPPORTED_MIME_TYPES, MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES, get_extension_from_mime
)
from app.documents.schemas import (
    DocumentUploadResponse,
    DocumentStatusResponse,
    DocumentListResponse,
    DocumentSectionsResponse,
    SectionItem,
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/projects", tags=["Documents"])


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Helper: resolve project and verify ownership                         #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

async def _get_project_or_404(db, project_id: str, owner_id: str):
    """
    Shared helper used by every document endpoint.
    Loads the project and verifies ownership.
    Returns 404 (never 403) if the project doesn't exist or belongs to another user.
    This prevents existence leakage.
    """
    project = await project_service.get_project(db, project_id, owner_id)
    if not project:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail={"code": "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND", "message": "Project not found"},
        )
    return project


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# POST /projects/{project_id}/documents                                #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

@router.post(
    "/{project_id}/documents",
    response_model=DocumentUploadResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_202_ACCEPTED,
    summary="Upload a document to a project",
)
async def upload_document(
    project_id: str,
    background_tasks: BackgroundTasks,
    file: UploadFile = File(..., description="PDF, DOCX, PPTX, XLSX, MD, or TXT file"),
    kind: DocumentKind = Query(
        DocumentKind.OTHER,
        description="Document type: BRD, PRD, TRD, or other"
    ),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """
    Upload a document to the project.

    - File is saved immediately â†’ returns document_id right away (202 Accepted)
    - Parsing + embedding runs in the background
    - Poll GET /projects/{project_id}/documents/{document_id} until status = ready
    - Duplicate uploads (same content) return the existing document_id with 200 OK
    - Returns 415 for unsupported MIME types
    - Returns 413 if file exceeds the size limit (50MB)
    """
    # Verify the project exists and belongs to this user
    await _get_project_or_404(db, project_id, current_user.id)

    # --- Validate MIME type ---
    mime_type = file.content_type or ""
    extension = get_extension_from_mime(mime_type)

    if not extension:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_415_UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE,
            detail={
                "code": "UNSUPPORTED_MIME_TYPE",
                "message": f"File type '{mime_type}' is not supported.",
                "details": f"Supported types: {list(SUPPORTED_MIME_TYPES.keys())}",
            },
        )

    # --- Read file content ---
    file_bytes = await file.read()

    # --- Validate file size ---
    if len(file_bytes) > MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_413_REQUEST_ENTITY_TOO_LARGE,
            detail={
                "code": "FILE_TOO_LARGE",
                "message": f"File exceeds the {MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES // (1024*1024)}MB size limit.",
                "details": f"Received {len(file_bytes)} bytes",
            },
        )

    # --- Handle upload (save file + create DB row) ---
    document = await doc_service.handle_upload(
        db=db,
        project_id=project_id,
        filename=file.filename or "upload",
        mime_type=mime_type,
        file_bytes=file_bytes,
        kind=kind,
    )

    # --- If already parsed (duplicate upload), return immediately ---
    if document.status == ParseStatus.READY:
        return DocumentUploadResponse(
            document_id=document.id,
            filename=document.filename,
            status=document.status,
            message="Duplicate file detected. Returning existing parsed document.",
        )

    # --- Schedule parse job as a background task ---
    background_tasks.add_task(
        doc_service.run_parse_job,
        document_id=document.id,
        project_id=project_id,
    )

    return DocumentUploadResponse(
        document_id=document.id,
        filename=document.filename,
        status=document.status,
        message="File uploaded successfully. Parsing started in the background.",
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# GET /projects/{project_id}/documents                                 #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

@router.get(
    "/{project_id}/documents",
    response_model=DocumentListResponse,
    summary="List all documents in a project",
)
async def list_documents(
    project_id: str,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """Return all documents uploaded to this project."""
    await _get_project_or_404(db, project_id, current_user.id)

    documents, total = await doc_service.list_documents(db, project_id)

    return DocumentListResponse(
        items=[DocumentStatusResponse.model_validate(d) for d in documents],
        total=total,
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# GET /projects/{project_id}/documents/{document_id}                  #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

@router.get(
    "/{project_id}/documents/{document_id}",
    response_model=DocumentStatusResponse,
    summary="Get document status and metadata",
)
async def get_document(
    project_id: str,
    document_id: str,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """
    Poll this endpoint after uploading a document.
    When status = 'ready', the document is fully parsed and embedded.
    When status = 'failed', check error_message for what went wrong.
    Returns 404 if the document_id doesn't belong to this project.
    """
    await _get_project_or_404(db, project_id, current_user.id)

    document = await doc_service.get_document(db, document_id, project_id)
    if not document:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail={
                "code": "DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND",
                "message": "Document not found in this project",
            },
        )

    return DocumentStatusResponse.model_validate(document)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# GET /projects/{project_id}/documents/{document_id}/sections         #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

@router.get(
    "/{project_id}/documents/{document_id}/sections",
    response_model=DocumentSectionsResponse,
    summary="Get the structured section outline of a parsed document",
)
async def get_document_sections(
    project_id: str,
    document_id: str,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """
    Returns the full list of parsed sections / chunks for a document.
    Only available after status = 'ready'.
    Useful for previewing what the agents will read during RAG.
    """
    await _get_project_or_404(db, project_id, current_user.id)

    document = await doc_service.get_document(db, document_id, project_id)
    if not document:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail={"code": "DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND", "message": "Document not found"},
        )

    if document.status != ParseStatus.READY:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail={
                "code": "DOCUMENT_NOT_READY",
                "message": f"Document is not ready yet. Current status: {document.status}",
            },
        )

    chunks = await doc_service.get_document_sections(db, document_id, project_id)

    sections = [
        SectionItem(
            section_id=chunk.section_id,
            section_title=chunk.section_title or f"Section {chunk.chunk_index + 1}",
            chunk_index=chunk.chunk_index,
            page=chunk.page,
            # Give a 200-character preview so the user can verify parsing quality
            text_preview=chunk.text[:200] + "..." if len(chunk.text) > 200 else chunk.text,
        )
        for chunk in chunks
    ]

    return DocumentSectionsResponse(
        document_id=document_id,
        filename=document.filename,
        sections=sections,
        total_sections=len(sections),
    )

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel
from app.documents.models import ParseStatus, DocumentKind


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Upload response                                                       #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

class DocumentUploadResponse(BaseModel):
    """
    Returned immediately after a file is uploaded (before parsing is done).
    The user should poll GET /documents/{id} until status = ready.
    """
    document_id: str
    filename: str
    status: ParseStatus
    message: str   # human-readable hint like "Parse job started"


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Document status (polling response)                                   #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

class DocumentStatusResponse(BaseModel):
    """
    Full document details â€” returned by GET /projects/{id}/documents/{doc_id}.
    The user polls this until status flips from 'parsing' to 'ready' or 'failed'.
    """
    id: str
    project_id: str
    filename: str
    extension: str
    mime_type: str
    file_size: int
    kind: DocumentKind
    status: ParseStatus
    section_count: int
    chunk_count: int
    error_message: Optional[str]
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime

    model_config = {"from_attributes": True}


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Document list                                                         #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

class DocumentListResponse(BaseModel):
    """Returned by GET /projects/{id}/documents"""
    items: list[DocumentStatusResponse]
    total: int


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Section outline                                                       #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

class SectionItem(BaseModel):
    """One entry in the document's section outline."""
    section_id: str
    section_title: str
    chunk_index: int
    page: int
    text_preview: str    # first 200 characters of the section text


class DocumentSectionsResponse(BaseModel):
    """
    Returned by GET /projects/{id}/documents/{doc_id}/sections.
    Shows the structured outline of the parsed document.
    """
    document_id: str
    filename: str
    sections: list[SectionItem]
    total_sections: int

import uuid
import structlog
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Optional

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy import select, func

from app.documents.models import Document, DocumentChunk, ParseStatus, DocumentKind
from app.documents.parser import parse_document, get_extension_from_mime
from app.storage.file_store import file_store
from app.storage.vector_store import document_store
from app.projects.models import Project, ProjectStatus

log = structlog.get_logger()


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Upload handling                                                       #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

async def handle_upload(
    db: AsyncSession,
    project_id: str,
    filename: str,
    mime_type: str,
    file_bytes: bytes,
    kind: DocumentKind = DocumentKind.OTHER,
) -> Document:
    """
    Step 1 of the pipeline: validate, hash-check, save file, create DB row.

    This runs synchronously in the request handler and returns immediately
    with status=pending. The actual parsing is triggered as a background task.
    """
    # Derive extension from MIME type
    extension = get_extension_from_mime(mime_type)
    if not extension:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported MIME type: {mime_type}")

    # Compute content hash for deduplication
    content_hash = file_store.compute_hash(file_bytes)

    # --- Idempotency check: same file already uploaded to this project? ---
    existing = await get_document_by_hash(db, project_id, content_hash)
    if existing:
        # Return the existing document instead of creating a duplicate
        log.info("Duplicate upload detected, returning existing document",
                 document_id=existing.id, project_id=project_id)
        return existing

    # Generate a new document ID
    document_id = str(uuid.uuid4())

    # Save raw file to disk: ./data/projects/{project_id}/uploads/{document_id}.{ext}
    file_path = await file_store.save_upload(
        project_id=project_id,
        document_id=document_id,
        extension=extension,
        content=file_bytes,
    )

    # Create the Document row in the DB with status=pending
    document = Document(
        id=document_id,
        project_id=project_id,
        filename=filename,
        extension=extension,
        mime_type=mime_type,
        file_size=len(file_bytes),
        content_hash=content_hash,
        file_path=file_path,
        kind=kind,
        status=ParseStatus.PENDING,
    )
    db.add(document)
    await db.commit()
    await db.refresh(document)

    log.info("Document uploaded", document_id=document_id,
             project_id=project_id, filename=filename)
    return document


async def get_document_by_hash(
    db: AsyncSession,
    project_id: str,
    content_hash: str,
) -> Optional[Document]:
    """Find a document in this project by its content hash (for deduplication)."""
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Document).where(
            Document.project_id == project_id,
            Document.content_hash == content_hash,
        )
    )
    return result.scalar_one_or_none()


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Background parse job                                                  #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

async def run_parse_job(document_id: str, project_id: str):
    """
    Step 2 of the pipeline: parse the file, chunk it, embed it.
    This runs in the background (via FastAPI BackgroundTasks).

    Flow:
      1. Load file bytes from disk
      2. Parse into chunks using parser.py
      3. Save chunks to DocumentChunk table in DB
      4. Embed chunks and upsert into ChromaDB
      5. Update Document status â†’ ready (or failed on error)
    """
    # We need a fresh DB session since this runs outside the request context
    from app.storage.database import AsyncSessionLocal

    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
        # Fetch the document row
        result = await db.execute(select(Document).where(Document.id == document_id))
        document = result.scalar_one_or_none()

        if not document:
            log.error("Document not found in parse job", document_id=document_id)
            return

        # Mark as parsing so the user can see progress when polling
        document.status = ParseStatus.PARSING
        document.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        await db.commit()

        try:
            log.info("Starting parse job", document_id=document_id,
                     filename=document.filename)

            # 1. Read the raw file from disk
            file_bytes = await file_store.read_bytes(document.file_path)

            # 2. Parse the file into chunks
            raw_chunks = parse_document(file_bytes, document.extension)

            if not raw_chunks:
                raise ValueError("Parser returned no chunks â€” file may be empty or unreadable")

            # 3. Save each chunk to the DocumentChunk table
            db_chunks = []
            for chunk in raw_chunks:
                db_chunk = DocumentChunk(
                    id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                    document_id=document_id,
                    project_id=project_id,
                    section_id=chunk["section_id"],
                    section_title=chunk["section_title"],
                    text=chunk["text"],
                    page=chunk["page"],
                    chunk_index=chunk["chunk_index"],
                    kind=document.kind.value,
                )
                db.add(db_chunk)
                db_chunks.append(db_chunk)

            await db.flush()  # write chunks to DB but don't commit yet

            # 4. Embed chunks and upsert into ChromaDB
            # Add the 'kind' field from the document to each chunk dict
            chroma_chunks = [
                {**c, "kind": document.kind.value}
                for c in raw_chunks
            ]
            document_store.upsert_chunks(
                project_id=project_id,
                document_id=document_id,
                chunks=chroma_chunks,
            )

            # 5. Update document stats and mark as ready
            document.status = ParseStatus.READY
            document.section_count = len(set(c["section_id"].split("_chunk")[0]
                                            for c in raw_chunks))
            document.chunk_count = len(raw_chunks)
            document.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            await db.commit()

            # Update the project status to 'ready' if it was still 'draft'
            await _maybe_mark_project_ready(db, project_id)

            log.info("Parse job complete", document_id=document_id,
                     chunk_count=document.chunk_count,
                     section_count=document.section_count)

        except Exception as e:
            # Save the error message so the user can see what went wrong
            await db.rollback()

            document.status = ParseStatus.FAILED
            document.error_message = str(e)
            document.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            await db.commit()

            log.error("Parse job failed", document_id=document_id, error=str(e))


async def _maybe_mark_project_ready(db: AsyncSession, project_id: str):
    """
    If the project is still in DRAFT status and now has at least one
    successfully parsed document, promote it to READY.
    This unlocks workflow triggers.
    """
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Project).where(Project.id == project_id)
    )
    project = result.scalar_one_or_none()

    if project and project.status == ProjectStatus.DRAFT:
        project.status = ProjectStatus.READY
        project.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        await db.commit()
        log.info("Project promoted to READY", project_id=project_id)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
# Read operations                                                       #
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ #

async def get_document(
    db: AsyncSession,
    document_id: str,
    project_id: str,
) -> Optional[Document]:
    """
    Fetch a document scoped to a project.
    Returns None if not found OR if it belongs to a different project â†’ 404.
    """
    result = await db.execute(
        select(Document).where(
            Document.id == document_id,
            Document.project_id == project_id,
        )
    )
    return result.scalar_one_or_none()


async def list_documents(
    db: AsyncSession,
    project_id: str,
) -> tuple[list[Document], int]:
    """Return all documents for a project, with total count."""
    query = select(Document).where(Document.project_id == project_id).order_by(
        Document.created_at.desc()
    )
    count_query = select(func.count()).select_from(Document).where(
        Document.project_id == project_id
    )

    results = await db.execute(query)
    count_result = await db.execute(count_query)

    return results.scalars().all(), count_result.scalar_one()


async def get_document_sections(
    db: AsyncSession,
    document_id: str,
    project_id: str,
) -> list[DocumentChunk]:
    """
    Return all chunks for a document, ordered by their position.
    Used to show the structured outline to the user.
    """
    result = await db.execute(
        select(DocumentChunk).where(
            DocumentChunk.document_id == document_id,
            DocumentChunk.project_id == project_id,
        ).order_by(DocumentChunk.chunk_index)
    )
    return result.scalars().all()

# Code Generated by Sidekick is for learning and experimentation purposes only.
"""
app/hitl/manager.py
-------------------
In-memory HITL event manager.

Responsibilities:
  1. push_to_run()  â€” stores the latest pending HITL request so that a
                      WebSocket connection can re-push it on reconnect.
  2. Provides asyncio.Queue per run for WebSocket consumers.
  3. deliver_client_response() â€” NOT needed; client answers are submitted
     via REST endpoints which call resume_with_hitl_answer() directly.

Trade-off: in-memory only â€” state lost on restart.
Production: replace with Redis pub/sub or a persistent queue.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# â”€â”€ Per-run queues â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
# Maps run_id â†’ asyncio.Queue of outbound WS messages (server â†’ client)
_run_queues:    Dict[str, asyncio.Queue] = {}

# Maps run_id â†’ last pending HITL request payload
# Used to re-push on WebSocket reconnect
_pending_hitl:  Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}


def _get_or_create_queue(run_id: str) -> asyncio.Queue:
    if run_id not in _run_queues:
        _run_queues[run_id] = asyncio.Queue(maxsize=50)
    return _run_queues[run_id]


async def push_to_run(run_id: str, message: Any) -> None:
    """
    Enqueue a server-to-client message for a run.
    Also caches the message as the current pending HITL request
    if it is a clarification_request or approval_request.

    Called by:
      - request_clarification node  (clarification_request)
      - approval_gate node          (approval_request)
      - complete_run node           (run_completed)
      - fail_run node               (error)
    """
    q = _get_or_create_queue(run_id)

    try:
        q.put_nowait(message)
    except asyncio.QueueFull:
        logger.warning("HITL queue full for run=%s â€” dropping message type=%s",
                       run_id, message.get("type") if isinstance(message, dict) else "?")

    # Cache pending HITL requests for reconnect re-push
    if isinstance(message, dict) and message.get("type") in (
        "clarification_request", "approval_request"
    ):
        _pending_hitl[run_id] = message
        logger.debug("Cached pending HITL request | run=%s | type=%s", run_id, message["type"])

    # Clear pending cache when run completes or errors
    if isinstance(message, dict) and message.get("type") in ("run_completed", "error"):
        _pending_hitl.pop(run_id, None)
        logger.debug("Cleared pending HITL cache | run=%s", run_id)


async def get_pending_hitl_request(run_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """
    Returns the last pending HITL request for a run (if any).
    Used by WebSocket handler to re-push the request on client reconnect.
    """
    return _pending_hitl.get(run_id)


async def get_run_queue(run_id: str) -> asyncio.Queue:
    """Returns the outbound message queue for a run (creates one if absent)."""
    return _get_or_create_queue(run_id)


def clear_run(run_id: str) -> None:
    """Clean up all in-memory state for a completed/failed run."""
    _run_queues.pop(run_id, None)
    _pending_hitl.pop(run_id, None)
    logger.debug("Cleared HITL manager state | run=%s", run_id)


# â”€â”€ Legacy function-level API (kept for backward compat) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

async def deliver_client_response(
    run_id: str,
    response: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
) -> None:
    """
    No-op stub.
    Client responses are submitted via REST endpoints (clarifications/approve/reject)
    which call resume_with_hitl_answer() directly â€” no queue needed for inbound messages.
    """
    logger.debug("deliver_client_response called | run=%s (no-op)", run_id)


async def request_clarification(
    run_id: str,
    questions: Optional[Any] = None,
) -> None:
    """Compatibility shim â€” delegates to push_to_run."""
    await push_to_run(run_id, {
        "type":      "clarification_request",
        "questions": questions,
    })


async def request_approval(
    run_id: str,
    payload: Optional[Any] = None,
) -> None:
    """Compatibility shim â€” delegates to push_to_run."""
    await push_to_run(run_id, {
        "type":    "approval_request",
        "payload": payload,
    })


# â”€â”€ Manager class (kept for existing imports) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

class HITLManager:
    async def push(self, run_id: str, event_type: str, data: Any = None) -> None:
        await push_to_run(run_id, {"type": event_type, "data": data})

    async def deliver(self, run_id: str, response: Any = None) -> None:
        await deliver_client_response(run_id, response)

    async def get_pending(self, run_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return await get_pending_hitl_request(run_id)


hitl_manager = HITLManager()

"""
app/hooks/node_hooks.py
Node hook decorator and usage recorder stubs.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import logging
import functools
from typing import Any, Callable, Optional

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def node_hook(name: str) -> Callable:
    """Decorator factory for node observability (SSE / cost tracking).
    Wraps the node function and passes through unchanged in this stub.
    """
    def decorator(fn: Callable) -> Callable:
        @functools.wraps(fn)
        async def wrapper(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
            logger.debug(f"node_hook enter: {name}")
            result = await fn(*args, **kwargs)
            logger.debug(f"node_hook exit: {name}")
            return result
        return wrapper
    return decorator


def record_usage(
    node_name: str,
    usage: Optional[Any] = None,
    run_id: Optional[str] = None,
) -> None:
    """Records token / cost usage for a node (stub â€” logs only)."""
    logger.debug(
        f"record_usage: {node_name}",
        extra={"run_id": run_id, "usage": usage},
    )
